+function findSavedEvidenceGalleryBlock(template: any) {
+  const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
+
+  for (const page of pages) {
+    const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+    const block = blocks.find((item: any) => item?.kind === "evidence-gallery");

+      return block;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return null;

+function ensureDefaultSignatureInFinalTemplate(template: any) {
+  const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
+
+  if (!pages.length) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const hasSignatureBlock = pages.some((page: any) => {
+    const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+    return blocks.some((block: any) => isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block));
+  });
+
+  if (hasSignatureBlock) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const lastPageIndex = pages.length - 1;
+
+  return {
+    ...template,
+    pages: pages.map((page: any, pageIndex: number) => {
+      if (pageIndex !== lastPageIndex) {
+        return page;
+      }
+
+      const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+      return {
+        ...page,
+        blocks: [...blocks, createDefaultFinalSignatureBlock(pageIndex)],
+      };
+    }),
+  };
+}
+
+function resolveSignatureText(value: any, context: Record<string, string>, fallback = "") {
+  const text = String(value || "").trim();
+
+  if (!text) {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+
+  return renderText(text, context) || fallback;

+function isSignatureOnlyFinalPage(page: any) {
+  const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+  return blocks.length > 0 && blocks.every((block: any) => isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block));

+function mergeSignatureOnlyFinalPagesIntoPrevious(template: any) {
+  const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
+
+  if (pages.length <= 1) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const mergedPages: any[] = [];
+
+  for (const page of pages) {
+    const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+    if (isSignatureOnlyFinalPage(page) && mergedPages.length > 0) {
+      const previousPage = mergedPages[mergedPages.length - 1];
+      const previousBlocks = Array.isArray(previousPage?.blocks) ? previousPage.blocks : [];
+
+      mergedPages[mergedPages.length - 1] = {
+        ...previousPage,
+        blocks: [
+          ...previousBlocks,
+          ...blocks.map((block: any, index: number) => ({
+            ...block,
+            id: block?.id || `merged-signature-${mergedPages.length}-${index + 1}`,
+            placement: block?.placement || "bottom",
+          })),
+        ],
+      };
+
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    mergedPages.push(page);
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...template,
+    pages: mergedPages,
+  };
+}

+function normalizeEvidenceLayoutValue(value: any) {
+  const text = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
+
+  if (!text || text === "auto") return "GRID_2X2";
+  if (text === "grid-2x2" || text === "grid_2x2") return "GRID_2X2";
+  if (text === "two-per-page" || text === "two_per_page" || text === "two-columns" || text === "two_columns") return "TWO_PER_PAGE";
+  if (text === "one-per-page" || text === "one_per_page") return "ONE_PER_PAGE";
+  if (text === "attachment-list" || text === "attachment_list") return "ATTACHMENT_LIST";
+  if (text === "stacked") return "STACKED";
+  if (text === "compact") return "COMPACT";
+
+  return value || "GRID_2X2";
+}
+
+function normalizeEvidenceBlockVariant(block: any, settings: any) {
+  const kind = normalizeFinalBlockKind(block?.kind || "");
+
+  if (kind === "evidence-gallery") {
+    return block?.variant || settings?.variant || settings?.style || "minimal";
+  }
+
+  return block?.variant || settings?.variant || settings?.style || "card";
+}

+function normalizeEvidenceShowCaptions(block: any, settings: any) {
+  if (typeof block?.evidenceShowCaptions === "boolean") {
+    return block.evidenceShowCaptions;
+  }
+
+  if (typeof settings?.evidenceShowCaptions === "boolean") {
+    return settings.evidenceShowCaptions;
+  }
+
+  if (typeof settings?.showCaptions === "boolean") {
+    return settings.showCaptions;
+  }
+
+  return false;

+
+function isStudioSignatureBlock(block: any) {
+  const kind = String(
+    block?.settings?.smartBlockKind ||
+      block?.smartBlockKind ||
+      block?.kind ||
+      "",
+  ).trim();
+
+  const title = String(block?.title || block?.settings?.title || "").trim();
+
+  return (
+    kind === "signature-grid" ||
+    kind === "signatures" ||
+    kind === "approval-signatures" ||
+    title.includes("╪¬┘ê┘é┘è╪╣") ||
+    title.includes("╪º╪╣╪¬┘à╪º╪»") ||
+    Array.isArray(block?.signatures)
+  );
+}
+
+function createDefaultStudioSignatureBlock() {
+  return {
+    id: "auto-default-signatures",
+    kind: "signature-grid",
+    title: "╪¬┘ê╪º┘é┘è╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪º╪╣╪¬┘à╪º╪»",
+    content: "",
+    variant: "minimal",
+    align: "center",
+    showTitle: false,
+    placement: "bottom",
+    visible: true,
+    autoDefaultSignature: true,
+    signatures: [
+      {
+        key: "counselor",
+        label: "╪º┘ä┘à┘ê╪¼┘ç ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪º╪¿┘è",
+        signerName: "{{identity.counselorName}}",
+        signerTitle: "┘à┘ê╪¼┘ç ╪╖┘ä╪º╪¿┘è",
+        imageUrl: "{{identity.counselorSignatureUrl}}",
+        required: false,
+      },
+      {
+        key: "principal",
+        label: "┘à╪»┘è╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à╪»╪▒╪│╪⌐",
+        signerName: "{{identity.principalName}}",
+        signerTitle: "┘à╪»┘è╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à╪»╪▒╪│╪⌐",
+        imageUrl: "{{identity.principalSignatureUrl}}",
+        required: false,
+      },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+function ensureDefaultStudioSignatureBlock(template: any) {
+  const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
+
+  if (!pages.length) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const hasSignature = pages.some((page: any) => {
+    const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+    return blocks.some((block: any) => isStudioSignatureBlock(block));
+  });
+
+  if (hasSignature) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const lastPageIndex = pages.length - 1;
+
+  return {
+    ...template,
+    pages: pages.map((page: any, pageIndex: number) => {
+      if (pageIndex !== lastPageIndex) {
+        return page;
+      }
+
+      const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+      return {
+        ...page,
+        blocks: [...blocks, createDefaultStudioSignatureBlock()],
+      };
+    }),
+  };
+}
+
+function isSignatureOnlyStudioPage(page: any) {
+  const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+  return blocks.length > 0 && blocks.every((block: any) => isStudioSignatureBlock(block));
+}
+
+function mergeSignatureOnlyStudioPagesIntoPrevious(template: any) {
+  const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
+
+  if (pages.length <= 1) {
+    return template;
+  }
+
+  const mergedPages: any[] = [];
+
+  for (const page of pages) {
+    const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
+
+    if (isSignatureOnlyStudioPage(page) && mergedPages.length > 0) {
+      const previousPage = mergedPages[mergedPages.length - 1];
+      const previousBlocks = Array.isArray(previousPage?.blocks) ? previousPage.blocks : [];
+
+      mergedPages[mergedPages.length - 1] = {
+        ...previousPage,
+        blocks: [
+          ...previousBlocks,
+          ...blocks.map((block: any, index: number) => ({
+            ...block,
+            id: block?.id || `merged-signature-${mergedPages.length}-${index + 1}`,
+            placement: block?.placement || "bottom",
+          })),
+        ],
+      };
+
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    mergedPages.push(page);
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...template,
+    pages: mergedPages,
+  };
+}
